Because a thing that **is not G** is that which actively undoes, reverses, or opposes G, this quadrant applies the **Adjoint Kernel Inversion Operator (Head F \<-\> Tail B)** so the action drives the system out of F back to B (e.g., move \-\> stop/halt/freeze; enter \-\> leave/expel; open \-\> shut/seal/lock; tie \-\> loosen/unknot/untie; fill \-\> drain/empty; grow \-\> shrink/compress).

   6. **The Dual Semantic Opposition Function \=DEFINE\_OPPOSITE(G):** Every gluon G \= (A: B \-\> F \[overcoming S\]) has two formal opposites: (a) **Active Vector Opposite \[is not / \-u, \+psi\] \= Adjoint Kernel Inversion (F \-\> B)** via active counter-work A^-1, and (b) **Passive Entropic Opposite \[was like / \-u, \-psi\] \= Strain Dominance (S \> A)** where G fails, stalls, or decays.  
4. **Triad Lockstep (3 x 3 x 3):** Every base invariant consists of exactly three sequential atomic actions, three resisting strains, and three resolved effects (Action\_n \-\> Strain\_n \-\> Effect\_n) with zero self-referential tautologies.  
5. **Substrate Neutrality (Plane-Collapse Ban):** The root definitive process, action, strain, and effect must never contain substrate jargon (physical, mental, emotional, biological, historical, social, logical, brain, skull, mouth, eyeball, computer).  
6. **Teleological Isolation:** Purpose clauses ('so that...', 'in order to...') belong strictly to Plane Q4 (Lyrical / Meaning), isolating existential intent from mechanical transitions.

8. **8-Modal Copula Dispersion & 5-Tier Negative/Positive Vector Extraction:** Around the origin **is (0, 0\)**, every gluon distributes flux across 8 modal copula channels — 4 Affirmative/Inclusive (**will\_be \[+W, Guaranteed\]**, **can\_be \[+A, Possible\]**, **is\_like \[+L, Metaphorical\]**, **may\_be \[+M, Plausible\]**) and 4 Negative/Exclusive (**is\_not \[-N, Precluded\]**, **cant\_be \[-X, Forbidden\]**, **wont\_be \[-O, Refused\]**, **was\_like \[-H, Regress\]**). This separates **Precluded (-N)** (simple boundary exclusion) from **Forbidden (-X)** (axiomatic contradiction or conservation violation).  
9. **SU(3) Color Confinement, White Singlet Hadron Baryons (r \+ g \+ b), & Non-Commutative 7-Plane Gauge Friction:** Every 3-step triad and 3-gluon Section 4 composite forms a color-neutral **White Singlet Hadron Baryon** balancing **Red (r: Initiating Will / Premise \+W)**, **Green (g: Mediating Operational Mechanism \+A/+L)**, and **Blue (b: Invariant Boundary Lock / Proof \-N/-X)** under the Cornell Confinement Potential V(r) \= \-alpha\_s / r \+ sigma \* r (critical string-snapping threshold r\_c \= 1.529). Across the 7 Planes (Q1..Q7), transitions along the constructive geodesic (**Q3 Physical \-\> Q5 Logical \-\> Q4 Lyrical \-\> Q1 Identity**) carry low gauge friction (0.8), whereas inverted leaps (**Q4 \-\> Q3** or **Q7 \-\> Q5**) incur a 2.5x gauge friction penalty requiring a bridging gluon operator.

* # **2\. The 5-Level Topological Closure Hierarchy (CEFR A1 Basis)**

* **Level 0 (Open Axis — 10 A1 Gluons):** Unconstrained movement and state change along an open dimension: **move, turn, shake, spread, flow, bend, break, start, mix, rise**.  
* **Level 1 (Wall / Threshold — 8 A1 Gluons):** Single boundary crossing, opening, or surface contact: **enter, leave, open, poke, rub, bounce, sort, wet**.  
* **Level 2 (Corner / Interface — 9 A1 Gluons):** Two-body meeting, joining, hitting, trading, or shaping: **stick, fit, hit, trade, cut, cross, match, gather, shape**.  
* **Level 3 (Pocket / Cradle — 6 A1 Gluons):** Active grip, tying, hanging, or pressing against slip: **hold, tie, hang, rest, twist, press**.  
* **Level 4 (Enclosure / Chamber — 7 A1 Gluons):** Full 3D containment, covering, filling, emptying, or sizing over time: **keep, cover, fill, empty, grow, shrink, trap**.

# **3\. The Compacted Invariant Basis (40 CEFR A1 Semantic Gluons)**

* ## **AEC-CORE-001: push-stillness-\>gone**

* **Invariant Triad:** push, step, go \-\> stillness, drag, distance \-\> shifted, carried, gone  
* **Definitive Process:** to take an unheld thing from one place to another along an open path against drag and distance  
* **Topological Closure:** Level 0 (Open Axis / Unconstrained Translation)  
* **Primary Invariant Label (CEFR A1 Semantic Gluon — is \[0, 0\]):** move  
* **Asymmetric Causal Kernel Head:** Ingress Tail B (Requirements): unanchored mass across an open path \| Egress Head F (Obligations): coordinate shift from x\_0 to x\_1 over elapsed time (K(A, B) \= min(head\_A, tail\_B))  
* **5-Tier Invariant Vector Associations:** Guaranteed (+W, Blue): position translation, kinetic velocity, distance traversed \| Forbidden (-X, Red): instantaneous teleportation across zero time, displacement without kinetic momentum  
* **Relative Action-Potential Vector Map (Direction x Magnitude from 'move'):**  
  * **Productive \-\> can be (+u, \+psi \| Inclusive \+ Latent Active):** \+1 walk / step, \+2 stride / march, \+3 sprint / bound  
  * **Reductive \-\> is not (-u, \+psi \| Latent Active \+ Exclusive \[F \-\> B\]):** \+1 slow / brake, \+2 stop / halt, \+3 freeze / immobilize  
  * **Constructive \-\> is like (+u, \-psi \| Latent Counteractive \+ Inclusive):** \+1 stroll / pace, \+2 glide / coast, \+3 cruise / migrate  
  * **Regress \-\> was like (-u, \-psi \| Exclusive \+ Latent Counteractive \[S \> A\]):** \+1 limp / hobble, \+2 plod / trudge / slog, \+3 crawl / stall  
* **7-Plane Projections:**  
  * **Q1 (Identity):** The self-directed shift of an agent or object along an open path.  
  * **Q2 (Possible):** An open path across space awaiting forward movement.  
  * **Q3 (Physical):** A person walking down a road or a boat gliding across open water.  
  * **Q4 (Lyrical):** Pushing forward across the open path so that distance is crossed and a new place is reached.  
  * **Q5 (Logical):** Position shift rule mapping starting point x\_0 to new point x\_1 over time t.  
  * **Q6 (Historical):** Footpaths, migration trails, and wheeled travel across land and sea.  
  * **Q7 (Emotive):** Free momentum, steady progress, and open-road purpose.

  * **Q2 (Possible):** A piece of wood set between the open jaws of a workbench vise.  
  * **Q3 (Physical):** A carpenter tightening a vise on a board or pressing flowers between heavy books.  
  * **Q4 (Lyrical):** Pushing the two jaws tight together so that the piece stays rock-still while worked on.  
  * **Q5 (Logical):** Opposed-jaw rule applying equal and opposite inward forces \+F and \-F.  
  * **Q6 (Historical):** Screw presses, workbench vises, and printing presses.  
  * **Q7 (Emotive):** Unshakeable grip, still focus, and solid control.

  * ## **AEC-CORE-034: put-loss-\>saved**

* **Invariant Triad:** put, guard, save \-\> loss, rot, waste \-\> put, guarded, saved  
* **Definitive Process:** to put good things inside a safe closed place so they stay whole for later use  
* **Topological Closure:** Level 4 (Enclosure / Chamber / Saving Over Time)  
* **Primary Invariant Label (CEFR A1 Semantic Gluon — is \[0, 0\]):** keep  
* **Asymmetric Causal Kernel Head:** Ingress Tail B (Requirements): valuable or volatile resource within an enclosed protective chamber across elapsed time \| Egress Head F (Obligations): passive custody and continuous preservation against external loss, rot, or unauthorized access  
* **5-Tier Invariant Vector Associations:** Guaranteed (+W, Blue): inventory continuity, barrier custody, protection against environmental decay \| Forbidden (-X, Red): custodial preservation across an open unshielded boundary, zero-loss retention across an actively breached perimeter  
* **Relative Action-Potential Vector Map (Direction x Magnitude from 'keep'):**  
  * **Productive \-\> can be (+u, \+psi \| Inclusive \+ Latent Active):** \+1 save / treasure, \+2 reserve / steward, \+3 endow / consecrate  
  * **Reductive \-\> is not (-u, \+psi \| Latent Active \+ Exclusive \[F \-\> B\]):** \+1 spend / discard, \+2 cast out / jettison, \+3 abandon /relinquish  
  * **Constructive \-\> is like (+u, \-psi \| Latent Counteractive \+ Inclusive):** \+1 store / stock, \+2 archive / bank, \+3 preserve / cache  
  * **Regress \-\> was like (-u, \-psi \| Exclusive \+ Latent Counteractive \[S \> A\]):** \+1 misplace / hoard, \+2 spoil / rot, \+3 squander / lose  
* **7-Plane Projections:**  
  * **Q1 (Identity):** The holding of useful things inside a safe room or jar across time.  
  * **Q2 (Possible):** Dry autumn grain ready to be poured into a sealed clay jar for winter.  
  * **Q3 (Physical):** Apples stored in a cool root cellar or tools kept in a locked chest.  
  * **Q4 (Lyrical):** Putting needed supplies inside a closed space so that they stay safe from rot and ready for tomorrow.  
  * **Q5 (Logical):** Conservation rule keeping stored stock S(t) \= S\_0 across time interval dt.  
  * **Q6 (Historical):** Clay grain jars, root cellars, and library archives.  
  * **Q7 (Emotive):** Steady foresight, quiet security, and readiness.

  * ## **AEC-CORE-035: wall-danger-\>safe**

* **Invariant Triad:** wall, roof, shield \-\> storm, danger, cold \-\> walled, roofed, safe  
* **Definitive Process:** to put a roof and walls all around a space to keep out wind, rain, and harm  
* **Topological Closure:** Level 4 (Enclosure / Chamber / All-Around Protection)  
* **Primary Invariant Label (CEFR A1 Semantic Gluon — is \[0, 0\]):** cover  
* **Asymmetric Causal Kernel Head:** Ingress Tail B (Requirements): exposed vulnerable surface or interior and impermeable protective barrier \| Egress Head F (Obligations): continuous shielding perimeter enclosing boundary against ambient weather or attack (K(A, B) \= min(head\_A, tail\_B))  
* **5-Tier Invariant Vector Associations:** Guaranteed (+W, Blue): interfacial barrier shielding, weatherproofing, exposure attenuation \| Forbidden (-X, Red): complete cover while leaving target exposed to line-of-sight attack, zero-thickness shielding against penetrating flux without absorption  
* **Relative Action-Potential Vector Map (Direction x Magnitude from 'cover'):**  
  * **Productive \-\> can be (+u, \+psi \| Inclusive \+ Latent Active):** \+1 roof / house, \+2 shelter / harbor, \+3 canopy / pavilion  
  * **Reductive \-\> is not (-u, \+psi \| Latent Active \+ Exclusive \[F \-\> B\]):** \+1 uncover / bare, \+2 peel / unroof, \+3 strip / expose  
  * **Constructive \-\> is like (+u, \-psi \| Latent Counteractive \+ Inclusive):** \+1 shield / screen, \+2 coat / wrap, \+3 insulate / blanket  
  * **Regress \-\> was like (-u, \-psi \| Exclusive \+ Latent Counteractive \[S \> A\]):** \+1 leak / gap, \+2 weather / fray, \+3 smother / collapse  
* **7-Plane Projections:**  
  * **Q1 (Identity):** The placing of a protective roof and walls around an inside space.  
  * **Q2 (Possible):** Travelers caught in cold rain looking for a dry roofed hut.  
  * **Q3 (Physical):** A stone cottage keeping a family warm and dry during a winter storm.  
  * **Q4 (Lyrical):** Placing walls and a roof around the inside room in order to keep out storm and danger.  
  * **Q5 (Logical):** Shielding rule blocking outside hazard flux F\_out so inside flux F\_in \-\> 0\.  
  * **Q6 (Historical):** Caves, thatched roofs, tents, and warm coats.  
  * **Q7 (Emotive):** Warm safety, dry comfort, and peaceful shelter.

  * ## **AEC-CORE-036: pour-emptiness-\>full**

* **Invariant Triad:** pour, pack, load \-\> emptiness, space, room \-\> poured, packed, full  
* **Definitive Process:** to put stuff into an empty cup, bag, or room until there is no empty space left  
* **Topological Closure:** Level 4 (Enclosure / Chamber / Making Full)  
* **Primary Invariant Label (CEFR A1 Semantic Gluon — is \[0, 0\]):** fill  
* **Asymmetric Causal Kernel Head:** Ingress Tail B (Requirements): hollow vessel, chamber, or matrix cavity with vacant interior volume \| Egress Head F (Obligations): state transition from vacant capacity to fully occupied interior volume (V\_void \-\> 0\)  
* **5-Tier Invariant Vector Associations:** Guaranteed (+W, Blue): volumetric occupancy, void reduction, brim equilibrium \| Forbidden (-X, Red): filling an open uncontained manifold without boundaries, occupation beyond maximum volume without compression  
* **Relative Action-Potential Vector Map (Direction x Magnitude from 'fill'):**  
  * **Productive \-\> can be (+u, \+psi \| Inclusive \+ Latent Active):** \+1 replenish / nourish, \+2 enrich / endow, \+3 abound / brim  
  * **Reductive \-\> is not (-u, \+psi \| Latent Active \+ Exclusive \[F \-\> B\]):** \+1 draw down / dip out, \+2 drain / tap, \+3 empty / exhaust  
  * **Constructive \-\> is like (+u, \-psi \| Latent Counteractive \+ Inclusive):** \+1 pack / load, \+2 charge / infuse, \+3 saturate / occupy  
  * **Regress \-\> was like (-u, \-psi \| Exclusive \+ Latent Counteractive \[S \> A\]):** \+1 stint / half-fill, \+2 cram / glut, \+3 spill / burst  
* **7-Plane Projections:**  
  * **Q1 (Identity):** The putting of matter into an empty container until it is full.  
  * **Q2 (Possible):** An empty water pitcher held beneath a flowing spring.  
  * **Q3 (Physical):** Pouring fresh water into a glass until it reaches the brim.  
  * **Q4 (Lyrical):** Pouring material into the empty vessel so that hollow space is turned into full supply.  
  * **Q5 (Logical):** Fullness ratio rule raising filled volume V\_used / V\_total \-\> 1\.  
  * **Q6 (Historical):** Water jugs, grain sacks, and cargo holds.  
  * **Q7 (Emotive):** Rich plenty, satisfied fullness, and generous supply.

  * ## **AEC-CORE-037: drain-fullness-\>bare**

* **Invariant Triad:** drain, clear, wipe \-\> fullness, weight, leftover \-\> drained, cleared, bare  
* **Definitive Process:** to take everything out of a full container or room until nothing is left inside  
* **Topological Closure:** Level 4 (Enclosure / Chamber / Making Empty)

* **Asymmetric Causal Kernel Head:** Ingress Tail B (Requirements): filled container or occupied chamber with unsealed egress aperture \| Egress Head F (Obligations): complete discharge of contents leaving vacant interior volume (V\_contents \-\> 0\)  
* **5-Tier Invariant Vector Associations:** Guaranteed (+W, Blue): content evacuation, volumetric clearance, unburdened capacity \| Forbidden (-X, Red): discharge from an empty vessel, emptying a chamber while its portal remains hermetically sealed  
* **Relative Action-Potential Vector Map (Direction x Magnitude from 'empty'):**  
  * **Productive \-\> can be (+u, \+psi \| Inclusive \+ Latent Active):** \+1 clear / clean out, \+2 unburden / free, \+3 purge / renew  
  * **Reductive \-\> is not (-u, \+psi \| Latent Active \+ Exclusive \[F \-\> B\]):** \+1 refill / top up, \+2 reload / restock, \+3 cram / stuff full  
  * **Constructive \-\> is like (+u, \-psi \| Latent Counteractive \+ Inclusive):** \+1 drain / pour out, \+2 void / vent, \+3 flush / decant  
  * **Regress \-\> was like (-u, \-psi \| Exclusive \+ Latent Counteractive \[S \> A\]):** \+1 dribble / leave dregs, \+2 leak dry / bleed out, \+3 gut / plunder  
* **7-Plane Projections:**  
  * **Q1 (Identity):** The taking out of all contents from a container until it is bare inside.  
  * **Q2 (Possible):** A full bucket of wash water ready to be tipped out and rinsed.  
  * **Q3 (Physical):** Pouring all the water out of a bucket or sweeping a room completely bare.  
  * **Q4 (Lyrical):** Taking out all old contents from the chamber so that clean empty space is ready again.  
  * **Q5 (Logical):** Zero-content rule reducing inside amount M\_in \-\> 0\.  
  * **Q6 (Historical):** Bilge pumps, drainage ditches, and vacuum jars.  
  * **Q7 (Emotive):** Clean slate, light relief, and open room.

  * ## **AEC-CORE-038: blow-tightness-\>wide**

* **Invariant Triad:** blow, stretch, swell \-\> tightness, wall, smallness \-\> blown, stretched, wide  
* **Definitive Process:** to push the walls of a closed bag or space outward from inside so it gets bigger  
* **Topological Closure:** Level 4 (Enclosure / Chamber / Growing Bigger)  
* **Primary Invariant Label (CEFR A1 Semantic Gluon — is \[0, 0\]):** grow  
* **Asymmetric Causal Kernel Head:** Ingress Tail B (Requirements): bounded internal entity with intake assimilation or internal pressure driving expansion \| Egress Head F (Obligations): increase in volumetric envelope, surface area, and operational complexity across duration  
* **5-Tier Invariant Vector Associations:** Guaranteed (+W, Blue): volumetric expansion, perimeter dilation, biomass/metric accretion \| Forbidden (-X, Red): growth without matter/energy assimilation, metric expansion that reduces boundary volume  
* **Relative Action-Potential Vector Map (Direction x Magnitude from 'grow'):**  
  * **Productive \-\> can be (+u, \+psi \| Inclusive \+ Latent Active):** \+1 bloom / expand, \+2 flourish / thrive, \+3 magnify / amplify  
  * **Reductive \-\> is not (-u, \+psi \| Latent Active \+ Exclusive \[F \-\> B\]):** \+1 pare / prune back, \+2 shrink / contract, \+3 compress / stunt  
  * **Constructive \-\> is like (+u, \-psi \| Latent Counteractive \+ Inclusive):** \+1 swell / widen, \+2 inflate / stretch, \+3 broaden / dilate  
  * **Regress \-\> was like (-u, \-psi \| Exclusive \+ Latent Counteractive \[S \> A\]):** \+1 bloat / puff up, \+2 blister / bulge, \+3 overblow / rupture  
* **7-Plane Projections:**  
  * **Q1 (Identity):** The outward pushing of a boundary to make the inside space larger.  
  * **Q2 (Possible):** A flat rubber balloon or rising bread dough ready to swell outward.  
  * **Q3 (Physical):** Blowing air into a balloon or yeast dough swelling warm in a bowl.  
  * **Q4 (Lyrical):** Pushing the enclosing walls outward so that the inside room grows to hold more.  
  * **Q5 (Logical):** Volume growth rule increasing enclosed size V\_1 \> V\_0.  
  * **Q6 (Historical):** Bread leavening, glassblowing, and bellows.  
  * **Q7 (Emotive):** Joyful growth, wide room, and rising life.

  * ## **AEC-CORE-039: squeeze-space-\>tight**

* **Invariant Triad:** squeeze, pack, crush \-\> space, softness, spread \-\> squeezed, packed, tight  
* **Definitive Process:** to push the walls of a space inward from outside so it gets smaller and firmer  
* **Topological Closure:** Level 4 (Enclosure / Chamber / Getting Smaller & Denser)  
* **Primary Invariant Label (CEFR A1 Semantic Gluon — is \[0, 0\]):** shrink  
* **Asymmetric Causal Kernel Head:** Ingress Tail B (Requirements): volumetric envelope containing compressible or cooling mass \| Egress Head F (Obligations): reduced perimeter, contracted coordinate span, and consolidated density  
* **5-Tier Invariant Vector Associations:** Guaranteed (+W, Blue): metric contraction, volume reduction, tightened surface area \| Forbidden (-X, Red): spontaneous volume collapse to negative metric span, shrinkage with zero mass/density change in an open gas  
* **Relative Action-Potential Vector Map (Direction x Magnitude from 'shrink'):**  
  * **Productive \-\> can be (+u, \+psi \| Inclusive \+ Latent Active):** \+1 trim / slim, \+2 condense / concentrate, \+3 compact / distill  
  * **Reductive \-\> is not (-u, \+psi \| Latent Active \+ Exclusive \[F \-\> B\]):** \+1 stretch / widen, \+2 swell / expand, \+3 dilate / balloon  
  * **Constructive \-\> is like (+u, \-psi \| Latent Counteractive \+ Inclusive):** \+1 press / pack, \+2 tighten / draw in, \+3 congeal / solidify  
  * **Regress \-\> was like (-u, \-psi \| Exclusive \+ Latent Counteractive \[S \> A\]):** \+1 waste / dwindle, \+2 wither / shrivel, \+3 collapse / vanish  
* **7-Plane Projections:**  
  * **Q1 (Identity):** The inward pushing of a boundary that makes a thing smaller and denser.  
  * **Q2 (Possible):** Loose snow or fluffy wool ready to be squeezed into a small firm ball.  
  * **Q3 (Physical):** Packing loose snow into a tight snowball or baling hay into a dense block.  
  * **Q4 (Lyrical):** Pushing the outer walls inward so that loose gaps vanish and the core becomes firm and compact.  
  * **Q5 (Logical):** Density rule shrinking volume V\_1 \< V\_0 while keeping mass M constant.  
  * **Q6 (Historical):** Hay balers, wool felting, and snow packing.  
  * **Q7 (Emotive):** Focused strength, solid firmness, and tight economy.

  * ## **AEC-CORE-040: surround-flight-\>locked**

* **Invariant Triad:** surround, shut, lock \-\> flight, gap, push \-\> surrounded, shut, locked  
* **Definitive Process:** to close walls all around a moving thing so it cannot get out  
* **Topological Closure:** Level 4 (Enclosure / Chamber / Closing In to Hold)  
* **Primary Invariant Label (CEFR A1 Semantic Gluon — is \[0, 0\]):** trap  
* **Asymmetric Causal Kernel Head:** Ingress Tail B (Requirements): mobile autonomous entity within reach and enclosing barrier perimeter with closure gate \| Egress Head F (Obligations): elimination of unconstrained degrees of freedom holding entity locked within boundaries  
* **5-Tier Invariant Vector Associations:** Guaranteed (+W, Blue): confinement, egress interdiction, kinetic freedom elimination \| Forbidden (-X, Red): escape across unbroken barrier perimeter without breach, trapping an unconfined entity across an open boundary  
* **Relative Action-Potential Vector Map (Direction x Magnitude from 'trap'):**  
  * **Productive \-\> can be (+u, \+psi \| Inclusive \+ Latent Active):** \+1 catch / net, \+2 harness / tame, \+3 contain / corral  
  * **Reductive \-\> is not (-u, \+psi \| Latent Active \+ Exclusive \[F \-\> B\]):** \+1 unpen / unlock, \+2 release / let out, \+3 free / liberate  
  * **Constructive \-\> is like (+u, \-psi \| Latent Counteractive \+ Inclusive):** \+1 pen / fold, \+2 enclose / impound, \+3 cage /coop  
  * **Regress \-\> was like (-u, \-psi \| Exclusive \+ Latent Counteractive \[S \> A\]):** \+1 snare / entangle, \+2 corner / maroon, \+3 entomb / suffocate  
* **7-Plane Projections:**  
  * **Q1 (Identity):** The closing of walls all around a moving thing to stop it from leaving.  
  * **Q2 (Possible):** A wild animal or loose spark inside an open pen just before the gate shuts.  
  * **Q3 (Physical):** Closing a fence gate around sheep in a pen or catching a mouse in a box trap.  
  * **Q4 (Lyrical):** Closing the surrounding walls tight so that an unruly or dangerous thing cannot run loose.  
  * **Q5 (Logical):** Boundary lock rule keeping position x strictly inside box domain D.  
  * **Q6 (Historical):** Animal corrals, fish traps, and fireboxes.  
  * **Q7 (Emotive):** Firm containment, closed borders, and controlled power.

10. # **4\. Formal Composite Child Nodes & Operational Algebra (Over CEFR A1 Gluons)**

11. Complex real-world concepts, specialized tools, and domain-specific verbs are NOT independent base invariants. They are formed by two algebraic operations over the 40 CEFR A1 Semantic Gluons:  
12. **1\. Directional-Magnitude Vector Displacement (Relative Action-Potential):** Shifting a single CEFR A1 Semantic Gluon (**is**) along one of the four Psochic Hegemony / Action-Potential quadrants (**Productive \[can be\]**, **Reductive \[is not\]**, **Constructive \[is like\]**, or **Regress \[was like\]**) by radial magnitude **\+1, \+2, or \+3** (e.g., **leave \[is\]** shifted along **Constructive (+u, \-psi)** to **\+3 \-\> egress / exude**, or along **Reductive (-u, \+psi)** to **\+3 \-\> expel / banish**).  
13. **2\. Multi-Gluon Intersection (\*) & Sequential Composition (o):** Combining two or more CEFR A1 Semantic Gluons across closure levels:

    * ## **Catalog of Verified Composite Algebraic Nodes (AEC-COMP-001 to AEC-COMP-245)**

* **AEC-COMP-001: library** \= keep (AEC-CORE-034) \* shape (AEC-CORE-027) \* trade (AEC-CORE-022)  
  * **Plain-English Decomposition:** Gathers recorded marks shaped onto pages (shape), keeps them safe in a room over time (keep), and lets people borrow and return them in turn (trade).  
* **AEC-COMP-002: clock** \= turn (AEC-CORE-002) \* shake (AEC-CORE-003) \* match (AEC-CORE-025)  
  * **Plain-English Decomposition:** Links a steady back-and-forth swing (shake) to turning wheels and hands (turn) so that people can match their actions to the time of day (match).  
* **AEC-COMP-003: blacksmith** \= start (AEC-CORE-008) o hit (AEC-CORE-021) o wet (AEC-CORE-018)  
  * **Plain-English Decomposition:** Lights a hot fire to soften iron (start), hits it with a hammer on an anvil (hit), and dips it into cool water to harden it (wet).  
* **AEC-COMP-004: knurl** \= rub (AEC-CORE-015) \* shape (AEC-CORE-027)  
  * **Plain-English Decomposition:** Presses and rubs a crisscross pattern of small ridges into a metal handle (rub \* shape) so a hand can hold it without slipping.  
* **AEC-COMP-005: fillet** \= shape (AEC-CORE-027) \[inside\_corner=round\]  
  * **Plain-English Decomposition:** Rounds out a sharp inside corner into a smooth curve (shape) so it does not crack under load.  
* **AEC-COMP-006: slog** \= move (AEC-CORE-001) \[quadrant=Regress (-u, \-psi), magnitude=+2, medium=deep\_mud\]  
  * **Plain-English Decomposition:** Moves forward slowly with heavy drag through sticky mud or wet ground (move).  
* **AEC-COMP-007: waddle** \= move (AEC-CORE-001) \* shake (AEC-CORE-003) \[side\_to\_side\]  
  * **Plain-English Decomposition:** Moves forward with short steps (move) while rocking weight from side to side (shake).  
* **AEC-COMP-008: dunk** \= wet (AEC-CORE-018) o move (AEC-CORE-001) \[down\_and\_up, brief\]  
  * **Plain-English Decomposition:** Dips a thing down into liquid for a moment (wet) and pulls it right back up (move).  
* **AEC-COMP-009: plink** \= hit (AEC-CORE-021) o shake (AEC-CORE-003) \[light\_weight, high\_ring\]  
  * **Plain-English Decomposition:** Hits a hard surface with a small light tap (hit) that makes a short, high ringing sound (shake).  
* **AEC-COMP-010: toddle** \= move (AEC-CORE-001) \* shake (AEC-CORE-003) \[unsteady\_balance\]  
  * **Plain-English Decomposition:** Takes short, unsteady steps forward (move) while wobbling back and forth to keep balance (shake).  
* **AEC-COMP-011: weld** \= start (AEC-CORE-008) o stick (AEC-CORE-019) \[melted\_edge\]  
  * **Plain-English Decomposition:** Uses intense heat to melt two metal edges (start) so they cool and stick together as one solid piece (stick).  
* **AEC-COMP-012: sieve** \= shake (AEC-CORE-003) \* sort (AEC-CORE-017)  
  * **Plain-English Decomposition:** Shakes a mesh screen from side to side (shake) so fine grains fall through while big lumps stay on top (sort).  
* **AEC-COMP-013: forge** \= start (AEC-CORE-008) o hit (AEC-CORE-021) o bend (AEC-CORE-006)  
  * **Plain-English Decomposition:** Heats metal until red-hot (start) and hammers it (hit) to bend and shape it without breaking (bend).  
* **AEC-COMP-014: vault** \= keep (AEC-CORE-034) \* cover (AEC-CORE-035) \* trap (AEC-CORE-040)  
  * **Plain-English Decomposition:** Keeps valuable things safe inside (keep) behind thick protective walls (cover) and locked doors that block thieves (trap).  
* **AEC-COMP-015: pendulum** \= hang (AEC-CORE-030) \* shake (AEC-CORE-003)  
  * **Plain-English Decomposition:** Hangs a weight from a high point (hang) so it swings smoothly back and forth at an even pace (shake).  
* **AEC-COMP-016: freeze / solidify** \= shrink \+ stick \+ rest \[cool or draw heat from a flowing mass \-\> pull its loose parts tightly together \-\> lock them into a firm, still block\]  
* **AEC-COMP-017: soak / absorb** \= enter \+ wet \+ hold \[draw outside flow across a porous border \-\> spread it through the inner spaces \-\> keep it inside the filled body\]  
* **AEC-COMP-018: know / verify** \= gather \+ match \+ hold \[take in raw signs from outside \-\> test and fit them against an inner pattern \-\> keep the proven match ready for use\]  
* **AEC-COMP-019: word / name** \= shape \+ tie \+ trade \[carve a distinct mark or sound \-\> bind it to a fixed meaning \-\> pass it across a gap to another agent\]  
* **AEC-COMP-020: aim / purpose** \= sort \+ turn \+ move \[pick one target end out of many \-\> point the carrier toward that mark \-\> drive action along the chosen path\]  
* **AEC-COMP-021: refresh / renew** \= empty \+ fill \+ start \[clear worn waste from a tired vessel \-\> pour fresh supply into the open space \-\> wake the restored carrier back to work\]  
* **AEC-COMP-022: drive / power** \= gather \+ press \+ move \[draw scattered force into a tight store \-\> release the pent push against a load \-\> move the heavy barrier forward\]  
* **AEC-COMP-023: endure / morale** \= tie \+ hold \+ move \[bind a group around a shared aim \-\> hold the line firm under heavy strain \-\> keep moving forward without breaking\]  
* **AEC-COMP-024: repair / moral** \= sort \+ trade \+ fit \[spot the harmful break between neighbours \-\> give back what was owed or taken \-\> fit the parted sides back into balance\]  
* **AEC-COMP-025: feed / sustenance** \= enter \+ break \+ grow \[take raw supply into an inner chamber \-\> break it down into usable parts \-\> build and keep the living frame strong\]  
* **AEC-COMP-026: identify / who** (formerly AEC-013) \= sort (AEC-CORE-017) \* match (AEC-CORE-025) \* hold (AEC-CORE-028)  
  * **Plain-English Decomposition:** Scans a crowded domain of active candidates (sort), matches observed traits against an established pattern (match), and holds the proven identity firm against doubt (hold).  
* **AEC-COMP-027: define / what** (formerly AEC-014) \= cut (AEC-CORE-023) \* sort (AEC-CORE-017) \* fit (AEC-CORE-020)  
  * **Plain-English Decomposition:** Traces clear boundary contours to part an entity from background noise (cut), sorts its internal properties by kind (sort), and fits it into an invariant category (fit).  
* **AEC-COMP-028: locate / where** (formerly AEC-015) \= cross (AEC-CORE-024) \* match (AEC-CORE-025) \* rest (AEC-CORE-031)  
  * **Plain-English Decomposition:** Measures two intersecting coordinate lines across open space (cross), matches the target intersection against an invariant reference datum (match), and rests a fixed marker on that site (rest).  
* **AEC-COMP-029: oscillate / swing** (formerly AEC-074) \= move (AEC-CORE-001) \* turn (AEC-CORE-002) \* bounce (AEC-CORE-016)  
  * **Plain-English Decomposition:** Displaces a hanging or anchored body away from resting center (move), lets restoring force swing it turning back (turn), and rebounds through the midpoint to repeat the cycle (bounce).  
* **AEC-COMP-030: strip / uncover** (formerly AEC-075) \= cut (AEC-CORE-023) o leave (AEC-CORE-012) o open (AEC-CORE-013)  
  * **Plain-English Decomposition:** Slits an outer protective sheath along its seam (cut), pulls the loose covering away (leave), and opens the raw interior core to clear view (open).  
* **AEC-COMP-031: dedicate / consecrate** (formerly AEC-076) \= sort (AEC-CORE-017) \* tie (AEC-CORE-029) \* keep (AEC-CORE-034)  
  * **Plain-English Decomposition:** Sets a chosen vessel or capacity apart from common use (sort), binds its action strictly to a sovereign purpose (tie), and keeps it untouched by extraneous tasks (keep).  
* **AEC-COMP-032: fly / glide** (formerly AEC-079) \= rise (AEC-CORE-010) \* flow (AEC-CORE-005) \* cross (AEC-CORE-024)  
  * **Plain-English Decomposition:** Lifts clear of ground contact on an aerodynamic pressure differential (rise), rides the smooth flow of an open fluid medium (flow), and crosses wide distances without surface drag (cross).  
* **AEC-COMP-033: sink / submerge** (formerly AEC-088) \= enter (AEC-CORE-011) o flow (AEC-CORE-005) o rest (AEC-CORE-031)  
  * **Plain-English Decomposition:** Passes downward through a liquid boundary under density excess (enter), yields along the downward potential gradient as fluid parts overhead (flow), and rests on the bottom substrate (rest).  
* **AEC-COMP-034: shear / offset** (formerly AEC-098) \= press (AEC-CORE-033) \* cut (AEC-CORE-023) \* move (AEC-CORE-001)  
  * **Plain-English Decomposition:** Exerts transverse normal force across a locked interface (press), overcomes cohesion along the slip plane (cut), and shifts the opposing sides out of planar alignment (move).  
* **AEC-COMP-035: mirror / reflect** (formerly AEC-101) \= hit (AEC-CORE-021) o bounce (AEC-CORE-016) o turn (AEC-CORE-002)  
  * **Plain-English Decomposition:** Intercepts an incoming wave packet on an impermeable boundary (hit), rebounds the wavefront without absorbing energy (bounce), and turns the exact image back across the field (turn).  
* **AEC-COMP-036: focus / concentrate** (formerly AEC-103) \= gather (AEC-CORE-026) \* shrink (AEC-CORE-039) \* poke (AEC-CORE-014)  
  * **Plain-English Decomposition:** Draws scattered rays or paths from a wide area inward (gather), squeezes their spread into a tight beam (shrink), and aims the sharp point at a single tiny mark (poke).  
* **AEC-COMP-037: damp / quiet** (formerly AEC-104) \= press (AEC-CORE-033) \* rub (AEC-CORE-015) \* rest (AEC-CORE-031)  
  * **Plain-English Decomposition:** Presses a soft pad against a shaking body (press), lets friction rub away the wild bouncing energy (rub), and brings the motion back to quiet stillness (rest).  
* **AEC-COMP-038: cleave / sunder** (formerly AEC-107) \= hit (AEC-CORE-021) o cut (AEC-CORE-023) o break (AEC-CORE-007)  
  * **Plain-English Decomposition:** Drives a sharp wedge deep into a seam with a heavy blow (hit), cuts through the bonded grain (cut), and splits the two halves completely apart (break).  
* **AEC-COMP-039: propel / thrust** (formerly AEC-108) \= start (AEC-CORE-008) o press (AEC-CORE-033) o move (AEC-CORE-001)  
  * **Plain-English Decomposition:** Wakes a strong surge of thrust (start), pushes hard against the resting medium (press), and drives the carrier steadily forward over the road (move).

  * **Plain-English Decomposition:** Catches a heavy body on an overhead line or floating cushion (hang), balances upward support to match downward weight exactly (match), and holds it poised in mid-air without touching the ground (rest).  
* **AEC-COMP-041: ground / baseline anchor** (formerly AEC-116) \= tie (AEC-CORE-029) o flow (AEC-CORE-005) o rest (AEC-CORE-031)  
  * **Plain-English Decomposition:** Binds an active line firmly to the solid earth (tie), lets stray buildup flow harmlessly away into the soil (flow), and holds the base level steady at zero (rest).  
* **AEC-COMP-042: synchronize / phase lock** (formerly AEC-118) \= turn (AEC-CORE-002) \* match (AEC-CORE-025) \* tie (AEC-CORE-029)  
  * **Plain-English Decomposition:** Tracks two repeating cyclic rhythms (turn), adjusts their pace until beats strike at the exact same moment (match), and locks their timing together so neither runs ahead (tie).  
* **AEC-COMP-043: condense / precipitate** (formerly AEC-122) \= shrink (AEC-CORE-039) o gather (AEC-CORE-026) o wet (AEC-CORE-018)  
  * **Plain-English Decomposition:** Chills warm vapor so its loose mist contracts (shrink), gathers microscopic droplets into clinging seeds (gather), and rains down as liquid water (wet).  
* **AEC-COMP-044: rectify / check-valve** (formerly AEC-138) \= open (AEC-CORE-013) \* trap (AEC-CORE-040) \* flow (AEC-CORE-005)  
  * **Plain-English Decomposition:** Swings wide to let forward flow rush through (open), snaps firmly shut against back-pressure to block any return (trap), and keeps the stream running in one direction only (flow).

  * **Plain-English Decomposition:** Pours intake into an open vessel until every corner is occupied (fill), packs the contents tight against the inner walls (press), and caps the brim so no further substance can enter (keep).

  * **Plain-English Decomposition:** Spins an inward fluid stream rapidly around a center axis (turn), draws the revolving liquid down a narrow funnel (flow), and tightens the spinning core into a fast hollow drain (shrink).

  * **Plain-English Decomposition:** Plunges a red-hot metal body into cold water (wet), draws heat out instantly so the lattice contracts before it can drift (shrink), and locks the hard crystal grain into frozen stillness (rest).

  * **Plain-English Decomposition:** Touches liquid against tiny dry pores (wet), pulls the liquid front through narrow microscopic tubes (poke), and lifts fluid upward against gravity to wet the dry cloth (rise).
